Let me try to be more precise. Let me look at what tools like TMHMM would likely predict.

TMHMM typically uses a hidden Markov model with:
- TM helix: ~19-21 residues average, high hydrophobicity
- Between TM: loops with lower hydrophobicity
- iN, iM, oM, oL: different loop types
